and Lemma
3.5
, shows that
\eqref{lt}_{2}
holds
for
0\leq t\leq T_{2}=\min\{T_{1},(1+Cc_{4})^{-12-2\nu}\}
.
∎

Lemma 3.7
.

For
T_{3}=\min\{T_{2},(1+Cc_{4})^{-20-8\nu}\}
and
t\in[0,T_{3}]
, it holds that

\begin{split}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}(|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{s}|^{2}_{2}+|\nabla l_{s}|^{2}_{2})\text{d}s\leq&M(c_{0}),\\
|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}(t)|_{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l(t)|_{2}+|l(t)|_{D^{2}}\leq&M(c_{0})c_{1}^{2\nu+1},\\
\int^{t}_{0}(|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{3}l|_{2}^{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}}+|l|^{2}_{D^{3}})\text{d}s\leq&M(c_{0})c_{1}^{2\nu+2}.\end{split}

(3.40)

Proof.

Applying
\partial_{t}
to
\eqref{ln}_{3}
yields

\begin{split}&h^{-\frac{1}{2}}l_{tt}-a_{4}w^{\nu}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\triangle l_{t}\\
=&-(h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}l_{t}-(h^{-\frac{1}{2}}v\cdot\nabla l)_{t}+a_{4}w^{\nu}_{t}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\triangle l+a_{4}w^{\nu}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}_{t}\triangle l\\
&+a_{5}(w^{\nu}ng^{\frac{3}{2}}H(v))_{t}+a_{6}(w^{\nu+1}h^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\psi)_{t}+\Pi(l,h,w,g)_{t}.\end{split}

(3.41)

Multiplying (
3.41
) by
w^{-\nu}l_{t}
, integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
and integration by parts lead to

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|^{2}_{2}+a_{4}|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{8}}\nabla l_{t}|^{2}_{2}\\
=&-\int\big((h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}l_{t}+(h^{-\frac{1}{2}}v\cdot\nabla l)_{t}\big)w^{-\nu}l_{t}\\
&+a_{4}\int(w^{\nu}_{t}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\triangle l+w^{\nu}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}_{t}\triangle l)w^{-\nu}l_{t}\\
&+\int\big(a_{5}(w^{\nu}ng^{\frac{3}{2}}H(v))_{t}+a_{6}(w^{\nu+1}h^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\psi)_{t}+\Pi(l,h,w,g)_{t}\big)w^{-\nu}l_{t}\\
&-a_{4}\int\nabla(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}l_{t}+\frac{1}{2}\int(w^{-\nu}h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}|l_{t}|^{2}=\sum^{6}_{i=1}J_{i},\end{split}

(3.42)

where
J_{i}
,
i=1,2,\cdots,6
, are given and estimated as follows:

\begin{split}J_{1}=&-\int\big((h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}l_{t}+(h^{-\frac{1}{2}}v\cdot\nabla l)_{t}\big)w^{-\nu}l_{t}\\
\leq&C|\varphi|_{\infty}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|^{2}_{2}+C|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}(|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{5}{4}}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|v|_{\infty}|\nabla l|_{2}\\
&+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|v_{t}|_{3}|\nabla l|_{6}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|v|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}|_{2})|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{2},\\
J_{2}=&a_{4}\int(w^{\nu}_{t}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\triangle l+w^{\nu}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}_{t}\triangle l)w^{-\nu}l_{t}\\
\leq&C(|w^{-1+\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|hg^{-1}|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{\infty}|g^{\frac{1}{4}}w_{t}|_{\infty}|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla^{2}l|_{2}\\
&+|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{4}}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l|_{2})|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{2},\\
J_{3}=&a_{5}\int(w^{\nu}ng^{\frac{3}{2}}H(v))_{t}w^{-\nu}l_{t}\\
\leq&C|hg^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}\Big((|w^{-1+\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|n|_{\infty}|w_{t}|_{6}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}+|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|n_{t}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{6})|g^{\frac{3}{4}}\nabla v|_{3}\\
&+|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|n|_{\infty}(|g_{t}|_{6}|g^{\frac{3}{4}}\nabla v|_{3}|\nabla v|_{\infty}+|g\nabla v|_{6}|g^{\frac{3}{4}}\nabla v_{t}|_{3})\Big)|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{2},\\
J_{4}=&a_{6}\int(w^{\nu+1}h^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\psi)_{t}w^{-\nu}l_{t}\\
\leq&C\big(|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|\nabla\psi|_{3}|w_{t}|_{6}+|w^{1+\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}(|\varphi|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}\nabla^{2}h|_{2}|h_{t}|_{\infty}\\
&+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|\nabla\psi_{t}|_{2})\big)|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{2},\\
J_{5}=&\int\Pi(l,h,w,g)_{t}w^{-\nu}l_{t}\\
\leq&C\big(|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}|hg^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|g^{\frac{1}{4}}w_{t}|_{6}|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{4}}|_{6}^{2}\\
&+|w^{1+\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}(|h_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{8}}|_{4}^{2}+|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{4}}|_{6}|\psi_{t}|_{3})\big)|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{2}\\
&+C\big(|w^{-1+\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\nabla l|_{6}|w_{t}|_{6}|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{4}}|_{6}+|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}(|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{2}}|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l|_{2}|h_{t}|_{\infty}\\
&+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}|_{2}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{4}}|\psi_{t}|_{3}|\sqrt{h}\nabla l|_{6})\\
\end{split}

(3.43)

\begin{split}&+|w^{-2+\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|hg^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|g^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla w|_{3}|\sqrt{g}\nabla w|_{\infty}|w_{t}|_{6}\\
&+|w^{-1+\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|hg^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}(|g^{-1}|_{\infty}|g_{t}|_{\infty}|\sqrt{g}\nabla w|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla w|_{2}\\
&+|\sqrt{g}\nabla w|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla w_{t}|_{2})\big)|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{2},\\
J_{6}=&-a_{4}\int\nabla(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\nabla l_{t}l_{t}+\frac{1}{2}\int(w^{-\nu}h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}|l_{t}|^{2}\\
\leq&C(|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}|_{2}+|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|\varphi|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|w_{t}|_{3}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{6})|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{2}\\
&+C|\varphi|_{\infty}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|^{2}_{2}.\end{split}

(3.44)

Integrating (
3.42
) over
(\tau,t)
(
\tau\in(0,t)
), using (
3.17
), Lemmas
3.3
-
3.6
, (
3.31
), (
3.39
) and

|g^{\frac{3}{4}}\nabla v_{t}|_{3}\leq C|\sqrt{g}\nabla v_{t}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}|g\nabla v_{t}|_{6}^{\frac{1}{2}},

(3.45)

one can obtain that for
0\leq t\leq T_{2}
,

\begin{split}&|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{\tau}|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{8}}\nabla l_{s}|^{2}_{2}\text{d}s\\
\leq&|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}(\tau)|^{2}_{2}+M(c_{0})c_{4}^{4\nu+8}\int^{t}_{\tau}(|\sqrt{g}\nabla^{2}w_{s}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{2}+1)|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{s}|^{2}_{2}\text{d}s\\
&+M(c_{0})c_{4}^{10+\nu}(t+t^{\frac{1}{2}}).\end{split}

(3.46)

Due to
\eqref{ln}_{3}
, one gets

\begin{split}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}(\tau)|_{2}\leq&\big(|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{4}}(-h^{-\frac{1}{2}}v\cdot\nabla l+a_{4}w^{\nu}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\triangle l\\
&+a_{5}w^{\nu}ng^{\frac{3}{2}}H(v)+a_{6}w^{\nu+1}h^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\psi+\Pi(l,h,w,g))|_{2}\big)(\tau),\end{split}

(3.47)

which, together with Lemma
3.1
, (
3.5
) and Remark
3.1
, implies that

\begin{split}&\limsup_{\tau\rightarrow 0}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}(\tau)|_{2}^{2}\\
\leq&C\big(|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}_{0}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}_{0}u_{0}\cdot\nabla l_{0}|_{2}+|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}_{0}h^{\frac{1}{4}}_{0}(h^{2}_{0}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\triangle l_{0}|_{2}\\
&+|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}_{0}h^{\frac{1}{4}}_{0}h_{0}^{b}h^{\frac{3}{2}}_{0}H(u_{0})|_{2}+|l^{1+\frac{\nu}{2}}_{0}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}_{0}\text{div}\psi_{0}|_{2}+|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}_{0}h^{\frac{1}{4}}_{0}\Pi(l_{0},h_{0},w_{0},g_{0})|_{2}\big)\\
\leq&C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}_{0}|_{\infty}(|l^{-\nu}_{0}|_{\infty}|\phi_{0}^{-\frac{\iota}{2}}|_{\infty}|u_{0}|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{0}|_{2}+|g_{5}|_{2}+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}|\phi_{0}^{-\iota}|_{\infty}|g_{5}|_{2}\\
&+|\phi_{0}^{2b\iota}|_{\infty}|\phi_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}\iota}\nabla u_{0}|_{3}|\phi_{0}^{2\iota}\nabla u_{0}|_{6})+C|l^{1+\frac{\nu}{2}}_{0}|_{\infty}(|\nabla^{2}\phi_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}\iota}|_{2}+|\nabla\phi_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}\iota}|_{4}^{2})\\
&+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}_{0}|_{\infty}|\nabla\phi_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}\iota}|_{6}|\nabla l_{0}|_{3}+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}-1}_{0}|_{\infty}|\phi_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}\iota}\nabla l_{0}|_{6}|\nabla l_{0}|_{3}\leq M(c_{0}).\end{split}

Letting
\tau\rightarrow 0
in
(
3.46
)
and using Gronwall’s inequality give that for
0\leq t\leq\min\{T_{2},(1+Cc_{4})^{-20-4\nu}\}
,

\begin{split}&|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}(|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{s}|^{2}_{2}+|\nabla l_{s}|^{2}_{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),\end{split}

(3.48)

which, along with (
3.39
), yields that for
0\leq t\leq\min\{T_{2},(1+Cc_{4})^{-20-4\nu}\}
,

|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}l(t)|_{D^{2}}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l(t)|_{2}+|l(t)|_{D^{2}}\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{2\nu+1}.

(3.49)

Next, to derive the
L^{2}
-estimates of
\nabla^{3}l
, one considers the
L^{2}
-estimates of

\big(\nabla\mathcal{E},\nabla\widetilde{F}=\nabla F(\nabla(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}},l-\bar{l})\big).

Using Lemmas
3.3
-
3.6
, (
3.17
) and (
3.37
)-(
3.2.2
),
one can get that

\begin{split}|\mathcal{E}|_{D^{1}_{*}}\leq&C\big(|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{5}{4}}|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{2}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{2}}|\psi|_{\infty}|v|_{3}|\nabla l|_{6}\\
&+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}(|v|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}l|_{2}+|\nabla v|_{3}|\nabla l|_{6})+|w^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|n|_{\infty}|\nabla w|_{6}|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla v|_{3}\\
&+|w^{\nu}|_{\infty}(|\nabla n|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla v|_{2}+|n|_{\infty}|\nabla g|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla v|_{3}|g\nabla v|_{6}\\
&+|n|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla^{2}v|_{2}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla w|_{6}|\nabla\psi|_{3})\\
&+|w^{\nu+1}|_{\infty}(|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{5}{4}}|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{4}}|_{6}|\nabla\psi|_{3}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla^{2}\psi|_{2})\\
&+|w^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{2}}|\psi|_{\infty}^{2}|\nabla w|_{2}+|w^{\nu+1}|_{\infty}(|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{5}{4}}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{8}}|_{4}^{2}\\
&+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{5}{4}}|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}\nabla^{2}h|_{2})+|w^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla l|_{6}|\nabla w|_{3}\\
&+|w^{\nu}|_{\infty}(|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{2}}|\psi|_{\infty}^{2}|\nabla l|_{2}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}l|_{2}\\
&+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla\psi|_{3}|\nabla l|_{6})+|w^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|\sqrt{g}\nabla w|_{6}|\nabla w|_{6}^{2}\\
&+|w^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}(|g^{-\frac{1}{2}}|_{\infty}|\nabla g|_{\infty}|\nabla w|_{4}^{2}+|\sqrt{g}\nabla w\cdot\nabla^{2}w|_{2})\big)\\
&\leq M(c_{0})(|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}+c_{1}^{3\nu+2}),\\
|\widetilde{F}|_{D^{1}_{*}}\leq&C(|\nabla(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}l|_{2}+|\nabla^{2}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}|_{3}|\nabla l|_{6}\\
&+|\nabla^{3}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}|_{2}|l-\bar{l}|_{\infty})\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{2\nu+1},\end{split}

(3.50)

where one has used (
3.5
) and the facts that, for
0\leq t\leq\min\{T_{2},(1+Cc_{4})^{-20-4\nu}\}
,

\begin{split}|g\nabla v|_{6}\leq&|h_{0}\nabla u_{0}|_{6}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}|(g\nabla v)_{s}|_{6}^{2}\text{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&C(|h_{0}\nabla^{2}u_{0}|_{2}+|\psi_{0}|_{\infty}|\nabla u_{0}|_{2})\\
&+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}(|g_{s}|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{6}+|g\nabla^{2}v_{s}|_{2}+|\nabla g|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{s}|_{2})^{2}\text{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(1+c_{4}^{2}(t+t^{\frac{1}{2}}))\leq M(c_{0}),\\
|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla^{2}v|_{2}\leq&|\sqrt{h_{0}}\nabla u_{0}|_{3}|h_{0}\nabla^{2}u_{0}|_{6}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}|(g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla^{2}v)_{s}|_{2}^{2}\text{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&|\sqrt{h_{0}}\nabla u_{0}|_{3}|h_{0}\nabla^{2}u_{0}|_{6}+Ct^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}\big(|g_{s}|^{2}_{\infty}|\sqrt{g}\nabla v|^{2}_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}v|^{2}_{2}\\
&+|g\nabla v_{s}|^{2}_{6}|\sqrt{g}\nabla^{2}v|^{2}_{3}+|g\nabla^{2}v_{s}|^{2}_{2}|\sqrt{g}\nabla v|^{2}_{\infty}\big)\text{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(1+c_{4}^{4}(t+t^{\frac{1}{2}}))\leq M(c_{0}),\\
\end{split}

(3.51)

\begin{split}|\sqrt{g}\nabla w|_{6}\leq&|\sqrt{h_{0}}\nabla l_{0}|_{6}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}|(\sqrt{g}\nabla w)_{s}|_{6}^{2}\text{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&C(|\sqrt{h_{0}}\nabla^{2}l_{0}|_{2}+|(h_{0})^{-1}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|\psi_{0}|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{0}|_{2})\\
&+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}(|g^{-1}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|g_{s}|_{\infty}|\nabla w|_{6}\\
&+|\sqrt{g}\nabla^{2}w_{s}|_{2}+|g^{-1}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|\nabla g|_{\infty}|\nabla w_{s}|_{2})^{2}\text{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(1+c_{4}^{3}(t+t^{\frac{1}{2}}))\leq M(c_{0}),\\
|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla v|_{3}\leq&C|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla v|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{2}|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla v|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{6}\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla v|_{2}+|\nabla g|_{\infty}|\varphi|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{3}|g\nabla v|_{6}\\
&+|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla^{2}v|_{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq M(c_{0})c_{1},\\
|\sqrt{g}\nabla w\cdot\nabla^{2}w|_{2}\leq&|\nabla l_{0}|_{\infty}|\sqrt{h_{0}}\nabla^{2}l_{0}|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}|(\sqrt{g}\nabla w\cdot\nabla^{2}w)_{s}|_{2}^{2}\text{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&|\nabla l_{0}|_{\infty}|\sqrt{h_{0}}\nabla^{2}l_{0}|_{2}+Ct^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}\big(|g_{s}|^{2}_{\infty}|g^{-1}|_{\infty}|\nabla w|^{2}_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}w|^{2}_{2}\\
&+|\sqrt{g}\nabla w_{s}|^{2}_{6}|\nabla^{2}w|^{2}_{3}+|\sqrt{g}\nabla^{2}w_{s}|^{2}_{2}|\nabla w|^{2}_{\infty}\big)\text{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(1+c_{4}^{4}(t+t^{\frac{1}{2}}))\leq M(c_{0}),\\
\|w\|_{D^{1}\cap D^{2}}\leq&\|l_{0}\|_{D^{1}\cap D^{2}}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}\|w_{s}\|^{2}_{D^{1}\cap D^{2}}\text{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(1+c_{4}^{2}(t+t^{\frac{1}{2}}))\leq M(c_{0}),\\
|\nabla^{3}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}|_{2}\leq&C(|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla^{3}h|_{2}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{5}{4}}|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}\nabla^{2}h|_{2}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{7}{4}}|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{4}}|_{6}^{3})\\
\leq&M(c_{0}).\end{split}

(3.52)

It follows from (
3.34
), (
3.49
)-(
3.50
), Lemma
4.3
and Lemmas
3.3
-
3.6
that

\begin{split}|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l-\bar{l})(t)|_{D^{3}}\leq&C(|w^{-\nu}\mathcal{E}|_{D^{1}_{*}}+|F(\nabla(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}},l-\bar{l})|_{D^{1}_{*}})\\
\leq&C(|w^{-\nu}|_{\infty}|\mathcal{E}|_{D^{1}_{*}}+|\nabla w^{-\nu}|_{3}|\mathcal{E}|_{6}+|\widetilde{F}|_{D^{1}_{*}})\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(c^{\nu+1}_{1}|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}+c_{1}^{4\nu+3}),\\
|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla^{3}l(t)|_{2}\leq&C(|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l(t)-\bar{l})|_{D^{3}}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{2}(|\nabla^{2}\psi|_{2}\\
&+|\nabla\psi|_{3}|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{4}}|_{6}+|\psi|_{\infty}^{2}+|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{4}}|_{6}^{3})(1+\|\nabla l\|_{1}))\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(c^{\nu+1}_{1}|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}+c_{1}^{4\nu+3}).\end{split}

(3.53)

Finally, one gets from (
3.48
), (
3.53
) and Lemma
3.4
that

\begin{split}\int^{t}_{0}(|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{3}l|_{2}^{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}}+|l|^{2}_{D^{3}})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{2\nu+2},\end{split}

(3.54)

for
0\leq t\leq T_{3}=\min\{T_{2},(1+Cc_{4})^{-20-8\nu}\}
.

The proof of Lemma
3.7
is completed.
∎

Lemma 3.8
.

For
T_{4}=\min\{T_{3},(1+Cc_{4})^{-40-10\nu}\}
and
t\in[0,T_{4}]
, it holds that

\begin{split}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+|\nabla l_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int^{t}_{0}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{ss}|_{2}^{2}ds\leq&M(c_{0}),\\
|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{3}l(t)|_{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l(t)|_{D^{1}_{*}}+|l(t)|_{D^{3}}\leq&M(c_{0})c_{1}^{4\nu+3},\\
\int^{t}_{0}(|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|\nabla^{2}l_{s}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq&M(c_{0})c^{3\nu}_{1}.\\
\end{split}

(3.55)

Proof.

Multiplying (
3.41
) by
w^{-\nu}l_{tt}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
yield

\begin{split}&\frac{a_{4}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{8}}\nabla l_{t}|^{2}_{2}+|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|^{2}_{2}=\sum^{12}_{i=7}J_{i},\end{split}

(3.56)

where
J_{i}
,

, are given and estimated as follows:

\begin{split}J_{7}=&-\int\big((h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}l_{t}+(h^{-\frac{1}{2}}v\cdot\nabla l)_{t}\big)w^{-\nu}l_{tt}\\

\end{split}

(3.57)

\begin{split}&+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}|_{2}+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|\psi_{t}|_{3}|\nabla l|_{6})\\

Finally, we derive the time weighted estimates for
l
, which will be used to show that the regular solution is actually a classical one. For simplicity, set

\displaystyle H^{t}(v)=

\displaystyle 4\alpha\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{i}v_{i}\partial_{itt}v_{i}+2\beta\text{div}v\text{div}v_{tt}+2\alpha\sum_{i\neq j}^{3}\partial_{i}v_{j}\partial_{itt}v_{j}

\displaystyle+2\alpha\sum_{i>j}(\partial_{itt}v_{j}\partial_{j}v_{i}+\partial_{i}v_{j}\partial_{jtt}v_{i}).

Lemma 3.9
.

For
T_{5}=\min\{T_{4},(1+M(c_{0})c_{5})^{-40-10\nu}\}
and

\begin{split}t^{\frac{1}{2}}|l_{t}(t)|_{D^{2}}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l_{t}(t)|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}(t)|_{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{\frac{\nu}{2}},&\\
\int^{t}_{0}s(|l_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),&\\
\frac{1}{2}c_{0}^{-1}\leq l(t,x)\leq\frac{3}{2}c_{0}\quad\text{for}\quad(t,x)\in[0,T_{5}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}.&\end{split}

(3.67)

Proof.

\partial_{t}
to

\begin{split}&h^{-\frac{1}{2}}l_{ttt}-a_{4}w^{\nu}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\triangle l_{tt}+2(h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}l_{tt}+(h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{tt}l_{t}+(h^{-\frac{1}{2}}v\cdot\nabla l)_{tt}\\
=&2a_{4}(w^{\nu}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}})_{t}\triangle l_{t}+2a_{4}(w^{\nu})_{t}((h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}})_{t}\triangle l\\
&+a_{4}(w^{\nu})_{tt}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\triangle l+a_{4}w^{\nu}((h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}})_{tt}\triangle l\\
&+a_{5}(w^{\nu}ng^{\frac{3}{2}}H(v))_{tt}+a_{6}(w^{\nu+1}h^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\psi)_{tt}+\Pi(l,h,w,g)_{tt}.\end{split}

(3.68)

Multiplying (

, integrating over

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|^{2}_{2}+a_{4}|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{8}}\nabla l_{tt}|^{2}_{2}\\

&+\int(2a_{4}(w^{\nu}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}})_{t}\triangle l_{t}+2a_{4}(w^{\nu})_{t}((h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}})_{t}\triangle l)w^{-\nu}l_{tt}\\
&+\int a_{4}(w^{\nu})_{tt}(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\triangle lw^{-\nu}l_{tt}\\

&+\int\big(a_{6}(w^{\nu+1}h^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\psi)_{tt}+\Pi(l,h,w,g)_{tt}\big)w^{-\nu}l_{tt}\\
\end{split}

(3.69)

\begin{split}&-a_{4}\int\nabla(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\nabla l_{tt}l_{tt}+\frac{1}{2}\int(w^{-\nu}h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}|l_{tt}|^{2}=\sum^{20}_{i=13}J_{i},\end{split}

(3.70)

where

,

\begin{split}J_{13}=&-\int\big(2(h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}l_{tt}+(h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{tt}l_{t}+(h^{-\frac{1}{2}}v\cdot\nabla l)_{tt}\big)w^{-\nu}l_{tt}\\
\leq&C|\varphi|_{\infty}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+C\big(|\varphi|^{2}_{\infty}|h_{t}|^{2}_{\infty}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{2}\\

&+|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\nabla l|_{3}|\varphi|^{\frac{5}{4}}_{\infty}|v|_{\infty}|h_{tt}|_{6}+|\varphi|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{\infty}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|v|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}|_{2}\\
&+|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\nabla l|_{\infty}(|\varphi|^{\frac{5}{4}}_{\infty}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|v_{t}|_{2}+|\varphi^{\frac{1}{4}}|_{\infty}|v_{tt}|_{2})\big)|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}\\

Lemma 3.10

\displaystyle M(c_{0}),

(3.78)

\displaystyle(|u|^{2}_{D^{2}}+|h\nabla^{2}u|^{2}_{2}+|u_{t}|^{2}_{2})(t)+\int^{t}_{0}(|u|_{D^{3}}^{2}+|u_{s}|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}})\text{d}s\leq

\leq&M(c_{0})c_{4}^{2}|\sqrt{h}\nabla u|_{2}^{2}+M(c_{0})c_{4}^{4}+\frac{1}{2}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{t}|^{2}_{2},\end{split}

(3.79)

which

(3.80)

3.9
,

\begin{split}|\mathcal{H}|_{D^{1}_{*}}\leq&C(|u_{t}|_{D^{1}_{*}}+|v|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}v|_{2}+|\nabla v|_{6}|\nabla v|_{3}+|l|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}\phi|_{2}+|\nabla\phi|_{3}|\nabla l|_{6}\\
&+|\phi|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}l|_{2}+|\nabla g|_{\infty}|\nabla l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{2}+|\nabla^{2}l^{\nu}|_{3}|g\nabla v|_{6}\\
&+|\nabla l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|g\nabla^{2}v|_{2}+|\nabla l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{2}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\nabla\psi|_{3}|\nabla v|_{6}\\
&+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}v|_{2})\leq M(c_{0})(|u_{t}|_{D^{1}_{*}}+c_{3}^{2\nu+3}),\\
|\widetilde{G}|_{D^{1}_{*}}\leq&C(|\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u|_{2}+|\nabla^{2}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}|_{3}|\nabla u|_{6}\\
&+|\nabla^{3}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}|_{2}|u|_{\infty})\leq M(c_{0}),\end{split}

(3.90)

where one has used the fact that

\begin{split}|\nabla^{3}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}|_{2}\leq&M(c_{0})(|\nabla h^{\frac{3}{4}}|^{3}_{6}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{5}{4}}+|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}\nabla^{2}h|_{2}|\psi|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{4}}+|\nabla^{3}h|_{2}).\end{split}

(3.91)

Hence, by (
3.80
)-(
3.82
), (
3.89
)-(
3.90
), Lemmas
3.3
-
3.4
and Lemma
4.3
, one has

\begin{split}|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u(t)|_{D^{3}}\leq&C|l^{-\nu}\mathcal{H}|_{D^{1}_{*}}+C|G(\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}},u)|_{D^{1}_{*}}\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(|u_{t}|_{D^{1}_{*}}+c_{3}^{2\nu+3}),\\
|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{3}u(t)|_{2}\leq&C(|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u(t)|_{D^{3}}+\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1,3}\cap D^{2}}\|u\|_{2})\\
&+C(1+\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1,3}\cap D^{2}}^{3}\|u\|_{1})(1+|\varphi|^{2}_{\infty})\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u(t)|_{D^{3}}+c_{3}^{2\nu+3}),\end{split}

(3.92)

which, along with
(
3.89
)
and Lemma
3.5
, yields
\eqref{2.61}_{2}
.
Then the proof is complete.

∎

We now turn to estimate the higher order derivatives of
u
.

Lemma 3.11
.

For
t\in[0,T_{5}]
, it holds that

\displaystyle|u(t)|_{D^{3}}+|h\nabla^{2}u(t)|_{D^{1}_{*}}\leq M(c_{0})c_{3}^{2\nu+3},

(3.93)

\displaystyle|u_{t}(t)|_{D^{1}_{*}}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{t}(t)|_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}(|u_{ss}|^{2}_{2}+|u_{s}|^{2}_{D^{2}})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle\int^{t}_{0}(|h\nabla^{2}u_{s}|^{2}_{2}+|u|^{2}_{D^{4}}+|h\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|(h\nabla^{2}u)_{s}|^{2}_{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}).

Proof.

Multiplying (
3.86
) by
l^{-\nu}u_{tt}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
lead to

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}(a_{2}\alpha|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla u_{t}|^{2}_{2}+a_{2}(\alpha+\beta)|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\text{div}u_{t}|^{2}_{2})+|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}=\sum^{4}_{i=1}I_{i},\end{split}

(3.94)

where
I_{i}
,
i=1,2,3,4
, are given and estimated as follows:

\begin{split}I_{1}=&\int l^{-\nu}\Big(-(v\cdot\nabla v)_{t}-(l\nabla\phi)_{t}-a_{1}(\phi\nabla l)_{t}\\
&-a_{2}(l^{\nu})_{t}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}Lu-a_{2}l^{\nu}\frac{h}{\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}}h_{t}Lu\Big)\cdot u_{tt}\end{split}

(3.95)

\begin{split}\leq&C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}\big(|v|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|v_{t}|_{2}|\nabla v|_{\infty}+|l_{t}|_{6}|\nabla\phi|_{3}+|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}|\phi|_{\infty}\\
&+|\phi_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla l|_{2}+|l|_{\infty}|\nabla\phi_{t}|_{2}\\
&+|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{6}|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{3}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u|_{2}\big)|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},\\
I_{2}=&\int l^{-\nu}\big(a_{2}g\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v)+a_{3}l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v)\big)_{t}\cdot u_{tt}\\
\leq&C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}\big(|(\nabla l^{\nu})_{t}|_{2}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}+|g_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla l^{\nu}|_{3}|\nabla v|_{6}\\
&+|\sqrt{h}\nabla l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|gh^{-1}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|\sqrt{g}\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{t}|_{2}\\
&+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi_{t}|_{2}|\nabla v|_{\infty}+|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{6}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{3}\big)|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},\\
I_{3}+I_{4}=&-\int a_{2}\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}Q(u_{t})\cdot u_{tt}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\int a_{2}\frac{h}{\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}}h_{t}(\alpha|\nabla u_{t}|^{2}+(\alpha+\beta)|\text{div}u_{t}|^{2})\\
\leq&C(|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\varphi|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{t}|_{2}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+|h_{t}|_{\infty}|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{t}|^{2}_{2}|\varphi|_{\infty}).\end{split}

(3.96)

Integrating
(
3.94
)
over
(\tau,t)
and using (
3.95
)-(
3.96
) yield that for
0\leq t\leq T_{5}
,

\begin{split}&|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{\tau}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{ss}|^{2}_{2}\text{d}s\\
\leq&C|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla u_{t}(\tau)|^{2}_{2}+M(c_{0})c_{4}^{2}\int^{t}_{0}|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{s}|^{2}_{2}\text{d}s+M(c_{0})(c_{4}^{7\nu+6}t+1),\end{split}

(3.97)

where (
3.17
), Lemmas
3.2
-
3.4
and
3.6
-
3.9
have been used.

It follows from the following fact

\begin{split}&\sqrt{h_{0}}l_{0}^{\nu}\Big(\sqrt{h_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla Lu_{0}+\frac{h_{0}}{\sqrt{h_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}}Lu_{0}\otimes\nabla h_{0}\Big)=\frac{l_{0}^{\nu}\sqrt{h_{0}}}{\sqrt{h_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}}\Big(\sqrt{h_{0}}g_{3}+\epsilon^{2}\nabla Lu_{0}\Big),\end{split}

\eqref{ln}_{2}
, (
3.3
),
(
3.5
)
, Lemma 3.1 and Remark 3.1 that

\begin{split}&\lim\sup_{\tau\rightarrow 0}|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{t}(\tau)|_{2}\leq\lim\sup_{\tau\rightarrow 0}|\sqrt{h}\nabla\mathcal{K}(\tau)|_{2}\\
\leq&C(|\phi_{0}^{\iota}u_{0}|_{6}|\nabla^{2}u_{0}|_{3}+|\nabla u_{0}|_{\infty}|\phi_{0}^{\iota}\nabla u_{0}|_{2}+|l_{0}|_{\infty}|\phi_{0}^{\iota}\nabla^{2}\phi_{0}|_{2}\\
&+|\nabla^{2}l_{0}\phi_{0}^{\iota+1}|_{2}+|\nabla l_{0}|_{3}|\phi_{0}^{\iota}\nabla\phi_{0}|_{6}+|l_{0}^{\nu}|_{\infty}(|\nabla\psi_{0}|_{3}|\phi_{0}^{\iota}\nabla u_{0}|_{6}\\
&+|\psi_{0}|_{\infty}|\phi^{\iota}_{0}\nabla^{2}u_{0}|_{2})+|l^{\nu-1}_{0}|_{\infty}|\psi_{0}|_{\infty}|\phi^{\iota}_{0}\nabla u_{0}|_{6}|\nabla l_{0}|_{3}\\
&+|l_{0}^{\nu}|_{\infty}|g_{3}|_{2}+|l_{0}^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\varphi_{0}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|\nabla^{3}u_{0}|_{2}+|l_{0}^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|h_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}Lu_{0}|_{6}|\nabla l_{0}|_{3}\\
&+|g_{2}|_{2}|\phi_{0}^{-\iota}|_{\infty}|l_{0}^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{0}|_{\infty}+|\sqrt{h_{0}}\nabla^{2}l_{0}^{\nu}|_{2}|h_{0}\nabla u_{0}|_{\infty}\\
&+|\sqrt{h_{0}}\nabla l_{0}^{\nu}|_{6}(|h_{0}\nabla^{2}u_{0}|_{3}+|\psi_{0}|_{\infty}|\nabla u_{0}|_{3}))\leq M(c_{0}).\end{split}

(3.98)

Letting
\tau\rightarrow 0
in (
3.97
), one gets from Gronwall’s inequality that for
0\leq t\leq T_{5}
,

\begin{split}&|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+|\nabla u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}|u_{ss}|^{2}_{2}\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),\end{split}

(3.99)

which, along with
(
3.92
)
, yields

|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u|_{D^{3}}+|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{3}u|_{2}+|h\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{1}}+|\nabla^{3}u|_{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{3}^{2\nu+3}.

(3.100)

Next, denote
\mathcal{G}=\mathcal{H}_{t}-a_{2}(l^{\nu}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}})_{t}Lu
. One notes that
(
3.86
)
gives

\begin{split}a_{2}L(\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u_{t})&=a_{2}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}Lu_{t}-a_{2}G(\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}},u_{t})\\
&=l^{-\nu}\mathcal{G}-a_{2}G(\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}},u_{t}).\end{split}

(3.101)

For deriving the
L^{2}
estimates of
(\nabla^{2}u_{t},\nabla^{4}u)
, one needs to estimate the
L^{2}
norm of
(\mathcal{G},\widehat{G}=G(\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}},u_{t}),\nabla^{2}\mathcal{H})
,
which follows from (
3.7
), (
3.17
), (
3.83
), (
3.99
)-(
3.100
) and Lemmas
3.2
-
3.10
that

\begin{split}|\mathcal{G}|_{2}\leq&C(|u_{tt}|_{2}+\|v\|_{2}|\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+\|l\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{2}}\|\phi_{t}\|_{1}+\|\phi\|_{2}|l_{t}|_{D^{1}}\\
&+|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{6}|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}Lu|_{3}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u|_{2}+|g_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla l^{\nu}|_{2}|\nabla v|_{\infty}\\
&+|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|\nabla(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{2}+|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|\sqrt{h}\nabla l|_{\infty}|gh^{-1}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|\sqrt{g}\nabla v_{t}|_{2}\\
&+|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{6}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{3}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi_{t}|_{2}|\nabla v|_{\infty}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{t}|_{2})\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(|u_{tt}|_{2}+c_{4}^{3\nu+3}),\\
|\mathcal{H}|_{D^{2}}\leq&C(|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+\|v\|_{2}\|\nabla v\|_{2}+\|l\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{3}}\|\nabla\phi\|_{2}\\
&+\|\nabla l^{\nu}\|_{2}(\|g\nabla v\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{2}}+\|\nabla g\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{2}}\|\nabla v\|_{2})\\
&+\|l^{\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{2}}\|\psi\|_{L^{q}\cap D^{1,3}\cap D^{2}}\|\nabla v\|_{2})\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c_{4}^{6\nu+5}),\\
|\widehat{G}|_{2}\leq&C(|\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}|_{\infty}|\nabla u_{t}|_{2}+|\nabla^{2}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}|_{3}|u_{t}|_{6})\leq M(c_{0}).\end{split}

(3.102)

It follows from (
3.81
)-(
3.82
), (
3.90
), (
3.99
)-(
3.102
), Lemmas
3.2
-
3.10
and Lemma
4.3
that

\begin{split}\displaystyle|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u_{t}|_{D^{2}}\leq&C|l^{-\nu}\mathcal{G}|_{2}+C|G(\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}},u_{t})|_{2}\\
\displaystyle\leq&M(c_{0})(|u_{tt}|_{2}+c_{4}^{3\nu+3}),\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}\leq&C(|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+|\nabla u_{t}|_{2}(|\psi|_{\infty}+|\nabla\psi|_{3})\\
&+|\psi|^{2}_{\infty}|u_{t}|_{2}|\varphi|_{\infty})\leq M(c_{0})(|u_{tt}|_{2}+c_{4}^{3\nu+3}),\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle|(h\nabla^{2}u)_{t}|_{2}\leq&C(|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+|h_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u|_{2})\leq M(c_{0})(|u_{tt}|_{2}+c_{4}^{3\nu+3}),\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle|u|_{D^{4}}\leq&C|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}}l^{-\nu}\mathcal{H}|_{D^{2}}\leq M(c_{0})(|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c_{4}^{6\nu+5})\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(|u_{tt}|_{2}+c_{4}^{6\nu+5}).\end{split}

(3.103)

Due to
\eqref{ln}_{2}
, it holds that for multi-index
\varsigma\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}_{+}
with
|\varsigma|=2
,

\begin{split}&a_{2}L(\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{\varsigma}u)=a_{2}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{\varsigma}Lu-a_{2}G(\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}},\nabla^{\varsigma}u)\\
=&\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{\varsigma}\big[\big(\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}})^{-1}l^{-\nu}\mathcal{H}\big]-a_{2}G(\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}},\nabla^{\varsigma}u),\\
\end{split}

(3.104)

which, along with (
3.82
)-(
3.83
), (
3.90
), (
3.99
)-(
3.100
), (
3.102
)-(
3.103
), Lemmas
3.2
-
3.10
and Lemma
4.3
, implies that

\begin{split}|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{2}u(t)|_{D^{2}}\leq&C|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{\varsigma}\big[\big(\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}})^{-1}l^{-\nu}\mathcal{H}\big]|_{2}\\
&+C(|\psi|_{\infty}|u|_{D^{3}}+|\nabla\psi|_{3}|\nabla^{2}u|_{6}+|\nabla^{2}u|_{2}|\psi|^{2}_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty})\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(|u_{tt}|_{2}+c_{4}^{6\nu+5}).\end{split}

(3.105)

At last, (
3.99
)-(
3.100
), (
3.103
), (
3.105
) and Lemma
3.5
yield (
3.93
).

The proof of this lemma is complete.
∎

Finally, the following time weighted estimates for the velocity
u
hold.

Lemma 3.12
.

For
t\in[0,T_{5}]
, it holds that

\displaystyle t|u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{D^{2}}+t|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+t|u_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+t|u(t)|^{2}_{D^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq M(c_{0})c^{6\nu+4}_{4},

(3.106)

\displaystyle\int^{t}_{0}s(|u_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2}+|u_{s}|^{2}_{D^{3}}+|\sqrt{h}u_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2})\text{\rm d}s

\displaystyle\leq M(c_{0})c^{6\nu+4}_{4}.

Proof.

Differentiating
(
3.86
)
with respect to
t
yields

\begin{split}&u_{ttt}+a_{2}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}l^{\nu}Lu_{tt}\\
=&-(v\cdot\nabla v)_{tt}-a_{1}(\phi\nabla l)_{tt}-(l\nabla\phi)_{tt}+a_{3}(l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v))_{tt}+a_{2}(g\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v))_{tt}\\
&-a_{2}(\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}l^{\nu})_{tt}Lu-2a_{2}(l^{\nu})_{t}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}Lu_{t}-2a_{2}\frac{h}{\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}}h_{t}l^{\nu}Lu_{t}.\end{split}

(3.107)

Multiplying
(
3.107
)
by
l^{-\nu}u_{tt}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
give

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|^{2}_{2}+a_{2}\alpha|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla u_{tt}|^{2}_{2}+a_{2}(\alpha+\beta)|(h^{2}+\epsilon^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\text{div}u_{tt}|^{2}_{2}=\sum^{8}_{i=5}I_{i},\end{split}

(3.108)

where
I_{i}
,
i=5,6,7,8
, are given and estimated as follows.

\begin{split}I_{5}=&\int l^{-\nu}\big(-(v\cdot\nabla v)_{tt}-a_{1}(\phi\nabla l)_{tt}-(l\nabla\phi)_{tt}\big)\cdot u_{tt}\\
\leq&C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}\big(|\nabla v_{t}|_{6}|v_{t}|_{3}+|\nabla v|_{\infty}|v_{tt}|_{2}+|v|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{tt}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{tt}|_{2}\\
&+|\phi_{tt}|_{2}|\nabla l|_{\infty}+|\phi_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}+|l_{t}|_{6}|\nabla\phi_{t}|_{3}\\
&+|l_{tt}|_{6}|\nabla\phi|_{3}+|l|_{\infty}|\nabla\phi_{tt}|_{2})|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},\\
I_{6}=&a_{3}\int l^{-\nu}(l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v))_{tt}\cdot u_{tt}\\
\leq&C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}(|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi_{tt}|_{2}|\nabla v|_{\infty}+|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|l_{tt}|_{6}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{3}\\
&+|l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|^{2}_{6}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{6}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{tt}|_{2}+|\psi_{t}|_{3}|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{6}|\nabla v|_{\infty}\\
&+|\psi|_{\infty}|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{6}|\nabla v_{t}|_{3}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi_{t}|_{3}|\nabla v_{t}|_{6})|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},\\
I_{7}=&a_{2}\int l^{-\nu}(g\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v))_{tt}\cdot u_{tt}\\
\leq&C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}\Big(|l^{\nu-3}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|l_{t}|^{2}_{6}|\nabla l|_{6}+|l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|l_{tt}|_{6}|\nabla l|_{3}\\
&+l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{6}|\nabla l_{t}|_{3}+|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}|g_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{\infty}\\
&+|l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{6}(|\nabla l|_{3}|g_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{\infty}+|\nabla l|_{6}|g\nabla v_{t}|_{6})\Big)|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}\\
&+C|l^{-1}|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}|g\nabla v_{t}|_{6}|u_{tt}|_{3}+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|\nabla l|_{3}|g_{tt}|_{6}|\nabla v|_{\infty}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}\\
&+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|gh^{-1}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|\sqrt{h}\nabla l|_{\infty}|\sqrt{g}\nabla v_{tt}|_{2}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}\\
&+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}(|\nabla l|_{\infty}|g_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{tt}|_{2})|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},\end{split}

(3.109)

\begin{split}I_{8}=&-a_{2}\int l^{-\nu}\big((\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}l^{\nu})_{tt}Lu+2(l^{\nu})_{t}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}Lu_{t}\\
&+\frac{2h}{\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}}h_{t}l^{\nu}Lu_{t}+l^{\nu}\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\cdot Q(u_{tt})\big)\cdot u_{tt}+\frac{1}{2}\int(l^{-\nu})_{t}|u_{tt}|^{2}\\
\leq&C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}\big(|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{6}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u|_{3}+|l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|^{2}_{6}|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{6}\\
&+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|h_{tt}|_{6}|\nabla^{2}u|_{3}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|h_{t}|^{2}_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u|_{2}\\
&+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{tt}|_{2}|\varphi|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}\big)|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}\\
&+C|l^{-1}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{6}|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}|u_{tt}|_{3}\\
&+C|l^{-1}|_{\infty}|l_{tt}|_{6}|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{2}|u_{tt}|_{3}+C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{6}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}|u_{tt}|_{3}.\end{split}

(3.110)

Multiplying
(
3.108
)
by
t
and integrating over
(\tau,t)
, one can obtain from the estimates on
I_{i}
(
i=5,...,8
), (
3.17
) and Lemmas
3.2
-
3.11
that

\begin{split}&t|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\frac{a_{2}\alpha}{4}\int^{t}_{\tau}s|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{ss}|^{2}_{2}\text{d}s\\
\leq&\tau|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}(\tau)|^{2}_{2}+M(c_{0})c^{6\nu+4}_{4}(1+t)+M(c_{0})c^{2\nu+8}_{5}\int^{t}_{\tau}s|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{ss}|^{2}_{2}\text{d}s.\end{split}

(3.111)

Due to
(
3.99
)
, there exists a sequence
s_{k}
such that

s_{k}\longrightarrow 0,\quad\text{and}\quad s_{k}|u_{tt}(s_{k},x)|^{2}_{2}\longrightarrow 0,\quad\text{as}\quad k\longrightarrow\infty.

Taking
\tau=s_{k}
and letting
k\rightarrow\infty
in
(
3.111
)
, one has by Gronwall’s inequality that

\begin{split}t|u_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+&\int^{t}_{0}s|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{ss}|^{2}_{2}\text{d}s+\int^{t}_{0}s|\nabla u_{ss}|^{2}_{2}\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0})c^{6\nu+4}_{4},\end{split}

(3.112)

for
0\leq t\leq T_{5}
, which, along with
(
3.103
)
and
(
3.112
)
, yields that

t^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla^{4}u(t)|_{2}\leq M(c_{0})c^{3\nu+2}_{4}.

(3.113)

Next, to derive the
L^{2}
estimate of
\nabla^{3}u_{t}
, one deals with the
L^{2}
estimates of

(\nabla\mathcal{G},\nabla\widehat{G}=\nabla G(\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}},u_{t})).

It follows from (
3.7
), (
3.83
), (
3.91
) and Lemmas
3.2
-
3.11
that

\begin{split}|\mathcal{G}|_{D^{1}_{*}}\leq&C(|u_{tt}|_{D^{1}_{*}}+\|\nabla v\|_{2}|\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|v|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}v_{t}|_{2}\\
&+\|l\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{3}}\|\phi_{t}\|_{2}+\|l_{t}\|_{D^{1}_{*}\cap D^{2}}\|\nabla\phi\|_{2}\\
&+\|l^{\nu-1}\|_{1,\infty}\|l_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{2}}(\|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}Lu\|_{1}+|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u|_{2})\\
&+(1+|\psi|_{\infty})(1+|\varphi|_{\infty})\|h_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{2}}\|l^{\nu}\|_{1,\infty}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{1}\\
&+\|g_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}}\|\nabla l^{\nu}\|_{2}\|\nabla v\|_{2}+\|\nabla l^{\nu}\|_{2}(|\nabla g|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|g\nabla^{2}v_{t}|_{2})\\
&+(|g\nabla v|_{\infty}+|\nabla g|_{\infty}\|\nabla v\|_{2}+\|g\nabla^{2}v\|_{1})\|l_{t}\|_{{D^{1}_{*}\cap D^{2}}}\|l^{\nu-1}\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{3}}\\
&+\|l^{\nu-1}\|_{1,\infty}\|l_{t}\|_{D^{1}_{*}}\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1,3}}\|\nabla v\|_{2}\\
&+\|l^{\nu}\|_{1,\infty}\|\psi_{t}\|_{1}\|\nabla v\|_{2}+\|l^{\nu}\|_{1,\infty}\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1,3}}\|\nabla v_{t}\|_{1})\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(|\nabla u_{tt}|_{2}+c^{4\nu+3}_{4}|g\nabla^{2}v_{t}|_{2}+c_{4}^{5\nu+5}|l_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c_{4}^{5\nu+7}),\end{split}

(3.114)

\begin{split}|\widehat{G}|_{D^{1}_{*}}\leq&C(|\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+|\nabla^{2}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}|_{3}|\nabla u_{t}|_{6}\\
&+|\nabla^{3}\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}|_{2}|u_{t}|_{\infty})\leq M(c_{0})(|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c^{2\nu+3}_{4}).\end{split}

(3.115)

Hence
(
3.101
)
, (
3.114
)-(
3.115
), Lemmas
4.3
and
3.2
-
3.11
yield that for
0\leq t\leq T_{5}
,

\begin{split}|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u_{t}|_{D^{3}}\leq&C|l^{-\nu}\mathcal{G}|_{D^{1}_{*}}+C|G(\nabla\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}},u_{t})|_{D^{1}_{*}}\\
\leq&M(c_{0})(\|u_{tt}\|_{1}+|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c_{4}^{6\nu+7}(|g\nabla^{2}v_{t}|_{2}+|l_{t}|_{D^{2}}+1)),\\
|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}\nabla^{3}u_{t}(t)|_{2}\leq&C(|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u_{t}|_{D^{3}}+|u_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}\psi|_{2}+|\nabla u_{t}|_{6}|\nabla\psi|_{3}\\
&+|\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}|\psi|_{\infty}+|\nabla u_{t}|_{2}\|\psi\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1,3}\cap D^{2}}|\varphi|_{\infty}+|u_{t}|_{2}|\psi|_{\infty}^{3}|\varphi|^{2}_{\infty})\\
\leq&C|\sqrt{h^{2}+\epsilon^{2}}u_{t}|_{D^{3}}+M(c_{0})(|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c_{4}^{2\nu+3}),\end{split}

which, along with
(
3.93
)
,
(
3.112
)
-
(
3.113
)
and Lemma
3.5
, yields
\eqref{2.70}_{2}
.

The proof of Lemma
3.12
is complete.
∎

It follows from Lemmas

-

that for

,

\displaystyle\|(\phi-\eta)(t)\|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}\cap D^{3}}+\|\phi_{t}(t)\|^{2}_{2}+|\phi_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}\|\phi_{ss}\|^{2}_{1}\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle Cc_{4}^{6},

\displaystyle\widetilde{C}^{-1}\leq gh^{-1}(t,x)\leq\widetilde{C},\ \ \ |\xi(t)|_{D^{1}_{*}}+|\zeta(t)|_{4}+|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}\nabla^{2}h(t)|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle|\nabla l(t)|^{2}_{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}(|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l|^{2}_{2}+|\nabla^{2}l|^{2}_{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}(|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{s}|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{3}l|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{3}l(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int^{t}_{0}(|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{ss}|_{2}^{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l_{s}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})c_{1}^{8\nu+6},

\displaystyle t|l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{D^{2}}+t|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+t|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})c^{\nu}_{1},

\displaystyle\int^{t}_{0}s(|l_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),\quad\frac{1}{2}c_{0}^{-1}\leq l(x,t)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}c_{0},

\displaystyle(|u|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}u|^{2}_{2}+|u_{t}|^{2}_{2})(t)+\int^{t}_{0}(|u|^{2}_{D^{3}}+|h\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2}+|u_{s}|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle(|u_{t}|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{t}|^{2}_{2}+|u|^{2}_{D^{3}}+|h\nabla^{2}u|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}})(t)+\int^{t}_{0}|u_{s}|^{2}_{D^{2}}\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})c^{2\nu+3}_{3},

\displaystyle\int^{t}_{0}(|u_{ss}|^{2}_{2}+|u|^{2}_{D^{4}}+|h\nabla^{2}u|^{2}_{D^{2}}+|(h\nabla^{2}u)_{s}|^{2}_{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle t|u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{D^{2}}+t|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+t|u_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+t|u(t)|^{2}_{D^{4}}\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})c^{6\nu+4}_{4},

\displaystyle\int^{t}_{0}s(|u_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}u_{s}|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h}u_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})c^{6\nu+4}_{4}.

Then setting

\begin{split}T^{*}=&\min\{T,(1+M(c_{0}))^{36\nu^{3}+104\nu^{2}+102\nu+36})^{-40-10\nu}\},\\
c_{1}^{2}=&M(c_{0})^{2},\quad c_{2}^{2}=c_{3}^{2}=M(c_{0})^{8\nu+7},\\
c_{4}^{2}=&M(c_{0})^{24\nu^{2}+45\nu+23},\quad c_{5}^{2}=M(c_{0})^{72\nu^{3}+207\nu^{2}+204\nu+70},\end{split}

one can arrive at the following desired estimates:

\displaystyle\|(\phi-\eta)(t)\|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}\cap D^{3}}+\|\phi_{t}(t)\|^{2}_{2}+|\phi_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}\|\phi_{ss}\|^{2}_{1}\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{5},

(3.116)

\displaystyle\|\psi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{q}\cap D^{1,3}\cap D^{2}}\leq c_{1},\hskip 5.69054pt|\psi_{t}(t)|_{2}\leq c^{2}_{4},\quad|h_{t}(t)|_{\infty}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{4},

\displaystyle h(t,x)>\frac{1}{2c_{0}},\ \frac{2}{3}\eta^{-2\iota}<\varphi,\ |\psi_{t}(t)|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}}+\int^{t}_{0}(|\psi_{ss}|^{2}_{2}+|h_{ss}|^{2}_{6})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{5},

\displaystyle\|n(t)\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1,q}\cap D^{1,4}\cap D^{1,6}\cap D^{2}\cap D^{3}}+|\xi(t)|_{D^{1}_{*}}+|\zeta(t)|_{4}+|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}\nabla^{2}h(t)|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle c_{1},

\displaystyle|n_{t}(t)|_{2}\leq c^{2}_{4},\quad|n_{t}(t)|_{\infty}+|\nabla n_{t}(t)|_{2}+|\nabla n_{t}(t)|_{6}+|n_{tt}(t)|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{5},

\displaystyle|u|^{2}_{\infty}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla u(t)|^{2}_{2}+\|u(t)\|^{2}_{1}+\int^{t}_{0}\big(\|\nabla u\|^{2}_{1}+|u_{s}|^{2}_{2}\big)\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{1},

\displaystyle|\nabla l(t)|^{2}_{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}(|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l|^{2}_{2}+|\nabla^{2}l|^{2}_{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{2},

\displaystyle|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{t}_{0}(|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{s}|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{3}l|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{2},

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{3}l(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int^{t}_{0}(|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{ss}|_{2}^{2}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l_{s}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{2},

\displaystyle t|l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{D^{2}}+t|\sqrt{h}\nabla^{2}l_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+t|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{2},

\displaystyle\int^{t}_{0}s(|l_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{1}^{2},\quad c_{1}^{-1}\leq l(t,x)\leq

\displaystyle c_{1},

\displaystyle(|u|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}u|^{2}_{2}+|u_{t}|^{2}_{2})(t)+\int^{t}_{0}(|u|^{2}_{D^{3}}+|h\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2}+|u_{s}|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{3},

\displaystyle(|u_{t}|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}}+|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{t}|^{2}_{2}+|u|^{2}_{D^{3}}+|h\nabla^{2}u|^{2}_{D^{1}_{*}})(t)+\int^{t}_{0}|u_{s}|^{2}_{D^{2}}\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{4},

\displaystyle\int^{t}_{0}(|u_{ss}|^{2}_{2}+|u|^{2}_{D^{4}}+|h\nabla^{2}u|^{2}_{D^{2}}+|(h\nabla^{2}u)_{s}|^{2}_{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{4},

\displaystyle t|u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{D^{2}}+t|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+t|u_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+t|u(t)|^{2}_{D^{4}}\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{5},

\displaystyle\int^{t}_{0}s(|u_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}u_{s}|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h}u_{ss}|_{D^{1}_{*}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq

\displaystyle c^{2}_{5}

for
0\leq t\leq T^{*}
, which are uniformly bounded with respect to both
\epsilon
and
\eta
.

3.3.
Vanishing of the artificial dissipations

By the uniform estimates (
3.116
), one can now obtain the local well-posedness of (
3.1
) with
\epsilon=0
and
\phi^{\eta}_{0}\geq\eta
for any constant
\eta>0
. For simplicity, denote
B_{R}
a ball centered at origin with radius
R
.

Lemma 3.13
.

Let
(
1.17
)
hold. Assume that
(\phi_{0},u_{0},l_{0},h_{0})
satisfy
(
2.10
)-(
2.11
), and there exists a constant
c_{0}>1
independent of
\eta
such that (
3.5
) holds. Then there exist a time
T^{*}>0
, independent of
\eta
, and a unique strong solution
(\phi^{\eta},u^{\eta},l^{\eta},h^{\eta})

in
[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
to (
3.1
) with
\epsilon=0
satisfying (
3.4
) with
T
replaced by
T^{*}
. Moreover, (
3.116
) hold for
(\phi^{\eta},u^{\eta},l^{\eta},h^{\eta})
uniformly (independent of
\eta
).

Proof.

First, it follows from Lemmas
3.1
–
3.12
that for every
\epsilon>0
and
\eta>0
, there exist a time
T^{*}>0
, independent of
(\epsilon,\eta)
, and a unique strong solution
(\phi^{\epsilon,\eta},u^{\epsilon,\eta},l^{\epsilon,\eta},h^{\epsilon,\eta})(t,x)
in
[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
to (
3.1
) satisfying the estimates in (
3.116
) which are independent of
(\epsilon,\eta)
.
Then by using of the characteristic method and the standard energy estimates for
(\ref {ln})_{4}
, one can show that for
0\leq t\leq T^{*}
,

|h^{\epsilon,\eta}(t)|_{\infty}+|\nabla h^{\epsilon,\eta}(t)|_{2}+|h^{\epsilon,\eta}_{t}(t)|_{2}\leq C(A,R,c_{v},\digamma,\eta,\alpha,\beta,\gamma,\delta,T^{*},c_{0}).

(3.117)

Thus, it follows from (
3.116
)-(
3.117
) and Lemma
4.2
that for any
R>0
, there exists a subsequence of solutions (still denoted by)
(\phi^{\epsilon,\eta},u^{\epsilon,\eta},l^{\epsilon,\eta},h^{\epsilon,\eta})
, which converges to a limit
(\phi^{\eta},u^{\eta},l^{\eta},h^{\eta})
as
\epsilon\rightarrow 0
in the following strong sense:

\begin{split}&(\phi^{\epsilon,\eta},u^{\epsilon,\eta},l^{\epsilon,\eta},h^{\epsilon,\eta})\rightarrow(\phi^{\eta},u^{\eta},l^{\eta},h^{\eta})\ \ \text{ in }\ C([0,T^{*}];H^{2}(B_{R})).\end{split}

(3.118)

Second, via the classical weak convergence arguments, one can easy show that
(\phi^{\eta},u^{\eta},l^{\eta},h^{\eta})
is the unique strong solution to (
3.1
) with
\epsilon=0
satisfying (
3.4
) with
T
replaced by
T^{*}
, and the uniform estimates (
3.116
).

Thus the proof of Lemma
3.13
is complete.

∎

3.4.
Nonlinear approximation solutions away from vacuum

In this subsection, we will prove the local well-posedness of the classical solution to the following Cauchy problem under the assumption that
\phi^{\eta}_{0}\geq\eta
:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\phi^{\eta}_{t}+u^{\eta}\cdot\nabla\phi^{\eta}+(\gamma-1)\phi^{\eta}\text{div}u^{\eta}=0,\\[2.0pt]
&u^{\eta}_{t}+u^{\eta}\cdot\nabla u^{\eta}+a_{1}\phi^{\eta}\nabla l^{\eta}+l^{\eta}\nabla\phi^{\eta}+a_{2}(l^{\eta})^{\nu}h^{\eta}Lu^{\eta}\\[2.0pt]
=&a_{2}h^{\eta}\nabla(l^{\eta})^{\nu}\cdot Q(u^{\eta})+a_{3}(l^{\eta})^{\nu}\psi^{\eta}\cdot Q(u^{\eta}),\\[2.0pt]
&(\phi^{\eta})^{-\iota}(l^{\eta}_{t}+u^{\eta}\cdot\nabla l^{\eta})-a_{4}(\phi^{\eta})^{\iota}(l^{\eta})^{\nu}\triangle l^{\eta}\\
=&a_{5}(l^{\eta})^{\nu}n^{\eta}(\phi^{\eta})^{3\iota}H(u^{\eta})+a_{6}(l^{\eta})^{\nu+1}(\phi^{\eta})^{-\iota}\text{div}\psi^{\eta}+\Theta(\phi^{\eta},l^{\eta},\psi^{\eta}),\\
&h^{\eta}_{t}+u^{\eta}\cdot\nabla h^{\eta}+(\delta-1)(\phi^{\eta})^{2\iota}\text{div}u^{\eta}=0,\\[2.0pt]
&(\phi^{\eta},u^{\eta},l^{\eta},h^{\eta})|_{t=0}=(\phi^{\eta}_{0},u^{\eta}_{0},l^{\eta}_{0},h_{0}^{\eta})=(\phi_{0}+\eta,u_{0},l_{0},(\phi_{0}+\eta)^{2\iota})\ \text{in}\ \mathbb{R}^{3},\\[2.0pt]
&(\phi^{\eta},u^{\eta},l^{\eta},h^{\eta})\rightarrow(\eta,0,\bar{l},\eta^{2\iota})\quad\text{as}\hskip 5.69054pt|x|\rightarrow\infty\quad\rm for\quad t\geq 0,\end{aligned}\right.

(3.119)

where
\psi^{\eta}=\frac{a\delta}{\delta-1}\nabla h^{\eta}
and
n^{\eta}=(ah^{\eta})^{b}
. For simplicity, in the rest of this subsection,
C
will denote a positive generic constant independent of
\eta
and
k
.

Theorem 3.1
.

Let
(
1.17
)
hold and
\eta>0
. Assume that the initial data
(\phi_{0},u_{0},l_{0},h_{0})
satisfy
(
2.10
)-(
2.11
), and (
3.5
) holds with a constant
c_{0}>0
independent of
\eta
. Then there exist a time
T_{*}>0
, independent of
\eta
, and a unique strong solution

(\phi^{\eta},u^{\eta},l^{\eta},h^{\eta}=\phi^{2\iota})

in
[0,T_{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
to (
3.119
) satisfying (
3.4
), and the uniform estimates (independent of
\eta
) (
3.116
) hold for
(\phi^{\eta},u^{\eta},l^{\eta},h^{\eta})
with
T^{*}
replaced by
T_{*}
.

The proof is given by an iteration scheme described below.

Let
(\phi^{0},u^{0},l^{0},h^{0})
be the solution to the following Cauchy problem

\left\{\begin{aligned} \displaystyle&U_{t}+u_{0}\cdot\nabla U=0,\ \ \text{in}\ \ (0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\[2.0pt]
&Y_{t}-W\triangle Y=0,\ \ \text{in}\ \ (0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&W^{-\frac{1}{2}}Z_{t}-W^{\frac{1}{2}}\triangle Z=0,\ \ \text{in}\ \ (0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&W_{t}+u_{0}\cdot\nabla W=0,\ \ \text{in}\ \ (0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&(U,Y,Z,W)|_{t=0}=(\phi^{\eta}_{0},u^{\eta}_{0},l^{\eta}_{0},h^{\eta}_{0})=(\phi_{0}+\eta,u_{0},l_{0},(\phi_{0}+\eta)^{2\iota})\ \ \text{in}\ \ \mathbb{R}^{3},\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&(U,Y,Z,W)\rightarrow(\eta,0,\bar{l},\eta^{2\iota})\quad\text{as}\hskip 5.69054pt|x|\rightarrow\infty\quad\rm for\quad t\geq 0.\end{aligned}\right.

(3.120)

Choose a time
\bar{T}\in(0,T^{*}]
small enough such that the uniform estimates (independent of
\eta
) (
3.116
) hold for
(\phi^{0},u^{0},l^{0},h^{0},\psi^{0}=\frac{a\delta}{\delta-1}\nabla h^{0})
with
T^{*}
replaced by
\bar{T}
.

Proof.

Step 1:
Existence. One starts with the initial iteration
(v,w,g)=(u^{0},l^{0},h^{0})
, and can obtain a classical solution
(\phi^{1},u^{1},l^{1},h^{1})
to (
3.1
) with
\epsilon=0
. Inductively,
given
(u^{k},l^{k},h^{k})
for
k\geq 1
, define
(\phi^{k+1},u^{k+1},l^{k+1},h^{k+1})
by solving the following problem:

\left\{\begin{aligned} \displaystyle&\phi^{k+1}_{t}+u^{k}\cdot\nabla\phi^{k+1}+(\gamma-1)\phi^{k+1}\text{div}u^{k}=0,\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}(u_{t}^{k+1}+u^{k}\cdot\nabla u^{k}+a_{1}\phi^{k+1}\nabla l^{k+1}+l^{k+1}\nabla\phi^{k+1})\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&+a_{2}h^{k+1}Lu^{k+1}=a_{2}(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}h^{k}\nabla(l^{k+1})^{\nu}\cdot Q(u^{k})+a_{3}\psi^{k+1}\cdot Q(u^{k}),\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}(l^{k+1}_{t}+u^{k}\cdot\nabla l^{k+1})-a_{4}(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{2}}(l^{k})^{\nu}\triangle l^{k+1}\\
=&a_{5}(l^{k})^{\nu}n^{k+1}(h^{k})^{\frac{3}{2}}H(u^{k})+a_{6}(l^{k})^{\nu+1}(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\psi^{k+1}+\Pi^{k+1},\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&h^{k+1}_{t}+u^{k}\cdot\nabla h^{k+1}+(\delta-1)h^{k}\text{div}u^{k}=0,\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&(\phi^{k+1},u^{k+1},l^{k+1},h^{k+1})|_{t=0}=(\phi^{\eta}_{0},u^{\eta}_{0},l^{\eta}_{0},h^{\eta}_{0})\\[2.0pt]
=&(\phi_{0}+\eta,u_{0},l_{0},(\phi_{0}+\eta)^{2\iota})\ \ \text{in}\ \ \mathbb{R}^{3},\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&(\phi^{k+1},u^{k+1},l^{k+1},h^{k+1})\longrightarrow(\eta,0,\bar{l},\eta^{2\iota})\quad\text{as}\hskip 5.69054pt|x|\rightarrow\infty\quad\rm for\quad t\geq 0,\end{aligned}\right.

(3.121)

where
\psi^{k+1}=\frac{a\delta}{\delta-1}\nabla h^{k+1}
,
n^{k+1}=(ah^{k+1})^{b}
and

\begin{split}\Pi^{k+1}=&a_{7}(l^{k})^{\nu+1}(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{3}{2}}\psi^{k+1}\cdot\psi^{k+1}+a_{8}(l^{k})^{\nu}(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l^{k+1}\cdot\psi^{k+1}\\
&+a_{9}(l^{k})^{\nu-1}(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l^{k}\cdot\nabla l^{k}.\end{split}

(3.122)

It follows from Lemma
3.13
with
(v,w,g)
replaced by
(u^{k},l^{k},h^{k})
and mathematical induction that one can solve
(
3.121
)
locally in time to get
(\phi^{k+1},u^{k+1},l^{k+1},\\
h^{k+1})
satisfying the uniform estimates (
3.116
). Moreover,
\psi^{k+1}
solves

\psi^{k+1}_{t}+\nabla(u^{k}\cdot\psi^{k+1})+(\delta-1)\psi^{k}\text{div}u^{k}+a\delta h^{k}\nabla\text{div}u^{k}=0.

(3.123)

To show the strong convergence of
(\phi^{k},u^{k},l^{k},\psi^{k})
, we set

\begin{split}&\bar{\phi}^{k+1}=\phi^{k+1}-\phi^{k},\ \ \bar{u}^{k+1}=u^{k+1}-u^{k},\ \ \bar{l}^{k+1}=l^{k+1}-l^{k},\ \ \\
&\bar{\psi}^{k+1}=\psi^{k+1}-\psi^{k},\ \ \bar{h}^{k+1}=h^{k+1}-h^{k},\ \ \bar{n}^{k+1}=n^{k+1}-n^{k}.\end{split}

Then (
3.121
) and (
3.123
) yield

\left\{\begin{aligned} \displaystyle&\bar{\phi}^{k+1}_{t}+u^{k}\cdot\nabla\bar{\phi}^{k+1}+\bar{u}^{k}\cdot\nabla\phi^{k}+(\gamma-1)(\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\text{div}u^{k}+\phi^{k}\text{div}\bar{u}^{k})=0,\\[4.0pt]
\displaystyle&(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}+a_{2}h^{k+1}L\bar{u}^{k+1}+a_{2}\bar{h}^{k+1}Lu^{k}=\sum_{i=1}^{4}\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{i},\\
\displaystyle\displaystyle&(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}-a_{4}\sqrt{h^{k+1}}(l^{k})^{\nu}\triangle\bar{l}^{k+1}=\sum_{i=1}^{4}\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{i}+\bar{\Pi}^{k+1},\\
\displaystyle\displaystyle&\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}+\nabla(u^{k}\cdot\bar{\psi}^{k+1}+\bar{u}^{k}\cdot\psi^{k})+(\delta-1)(\bar{\psi}^{k}\text{div}u^{k}+\psi^{k-1}\text{div}\bar{u}^{k})\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&+a\delta(h^{k}\nabla\text{div}\bar{u}^{k}+\bar{h}^{k}\nabla\text{div}u^{k-1})=0,\\[4.0pt]
\displaystyle&(\bar{\phi}^{k+1},\bar{u}^{k+1},\bar{l}^{k+1},\bar{\psi}^{k+1})|_{t=0}=(0,0,0,0)\ \ \text{in}\ \ \mathbb{R}^{3},\\[4.0pt]
\displaystyle&(\bar{\phi}^{k+1},\bar{u}^{k+1},\bar{l}^{k+1},\bar{\psi}^{k+1})\longrightarrow(0,0,0,0)\quad\text{as}\ \ |x|\rightarrow\infty\quad\rm for\quad t\geq 0,\end{aligned}\right.

(3.124)

where

\begin{split}\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{1}=&-(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}(u^{k}\cdot\nabla\bar{u}^{k}+\bar{u}^{k}\cdot\nabla u^{k-1})\\
&-\big((l^{k+1})^{-\nu}-(l^{k})^{-\nu}\big)(u_{t}^{k}+u^{k-1}\cdot\nabla u^{k-1}),\\
\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{2}=&-(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}(a_{1}\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\nabla l^{k+1}+a_{1}\phi^{k}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}+\bar{l}^{k+1}\nabla\phi^{k+1}+l^{k}\nabla\bar{\phi}^{k+1})\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&-\big((l^{k+1})^{-\nu}-(l^{k})^{-\nu}\big)(a_{1}\phi^{k}\nabla l^{k}+l^{k}\nabla\phi^{k}),\\
\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{3}=&a_{2}(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}\Big(h^{k}\big(\nabla(l^{k+1})^{\nu}-\nabla(l^{k})^{\nu}\big)\cdot Q(u^{k})+h^{k}\nabla(l^{k})^{\nu}\cdot Q(\bar{u}^{k})\\[2.0pt]
\displaystyle&+\bar{h}^{k}\nabla(l^{k})^{\nu}\cdot Q(u^{k-1})\Big)+a_{3}\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\cdot Q(u^{k})+a_{3}\psi^{k}\cdot Q(\bar{u}^{k}),\\
\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{4}=&a_{2}\big((l^{k+1})^{-\nu}-(l^{k})^{-\nu}\big)h^{k-1}\nabla(l^{k})^{\nu}\cdot Q(u^{k-1}),\\
\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{1}=&-(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}(u^{k}\cdot\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}+\bar{u}^{k}\cdot\nabla l^{k})\\
&-((h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}-(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{2}})(l^{k}_{t}+u^{k-1}\cdot\nabla l^{k}),\\
\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{2}=&a_{4}\big(\sqrt{h^{k+1}}((l^{k})^{\nu}-(l^{k-1})^{\nu})+(\sqrt{h^{k+1}}-\sqrt{h^{k}})(l^{k-1})^{\nu}\big)\triangle l^{k},\\
\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{3}=&a_{5}(l^{k})^{\nu}n^{k+1}\big((h^{k})^{\frac{3}{2}}(H(u^{k})-H(u^{k-1}))+((h^{k})^{\frac{3}{2}}-(h^{k-1})^{\frac{3}{2}})H(u^{k-1})\big)\\
\displaystyle&+a_{5}(h^{k-1})^{\frac{3}{2}}H(u^{k-1})\big((l^{k})^{\nu}\bar{n}^{k+1}+((l^{k})^{\nu}-(l^{k-1})^{\nu})n^{k}\big),\\
\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{4}=&a_{6}(l^{k})^{\nu+1}\big((h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\bar{\psi}^{k+1}+((h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}-(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{2}})\text{div}\psi^{k}\big)\\
&+a_{6}((l^{k})^{\nu+1}-(l^{k-1})^{\nu+1})(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\psi^{k},\\
\displaystyle\bar{\Pi}^{k+1}=&a_{7}(l^{k})^{\nu+1}((h^{k+1})^{-\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\cdot(\psi^{k+1}+\psi^{k})+((h^{k+1})^{-\frac{3}{2}}-(h^{k})^{-\frac{3}{2}})\psi^{k}\cdot\psi^{k})\\
&+a_{7}((l^{k})^{\nu+1}-(l^{k-1})^{\nu+1})(h^{k})^{-\frac{3}{2}}\psi^{k}\cdot\psi^{k}\\
&+a_{8}(l^{k})^{\nu}(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\nabla l^{k+1}\cdot\bar{\psi}^{k+1}+\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}\cdot\psi^{k})\end{split}

\begin{split}&+a_{8}\big((l^{k})^{\nu}((h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}-(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{2}})+((l^{k})^{\nu}-(l^{k-1})^{\nu})(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\nabla l^{k}\cdot\psi^{k}\\
&+a_{9}(l^{k})^{\nu-1}\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}\cdot(\nabla l^{k}+\nabla l^{k-1})\\
&+a_{9}\big((l^{k})^{\nu-1}(\sqrt{h^{k}}-\sqrt{h^{k-1}})+\sqrt{h^{k-1}}((l^{k})^{\nu-1}-(l^{k-1})^{\nu-1}))|\nabla l^{k-1}|^{2}.\end{split}

Next, starting from (
3.124
), one will show that
\{(\phi^{k},u^{k},l^{k},\psi^{k})\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
is actually a Cauchy sequence in proper functional spaces, which requires some
estimates for

\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\in H^{2}
,
\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\in H^{1}
, and
(\bar{u}^{k+1},\bar{l}^{k+1})
in some suitable weighted
H^{2}
spaces. For this purpose, one first needs the following lemma.

Lemma 3.14
.

\begin{split}(\bar{h}^{k+1},\ \bar{\phi}^{k+1})\in L^{\infty}([0,\bar{T}];H^{3})\quad\text{and }\quad\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\in L^{\infty}([0,\bar{T}];H^{2})\quad\text{for}\quad k=1,2,....\end{split}

The proof follows from the same argument for Lemma 4.4 of
[
8
]
. This lemma helps to deal with some singular terms of type
\infty-\infty
such as
a_{2}\bar{h}^{k+1}Lu^{k}
in
\eqref{k+3}_{2}
.

Step 1.1:
Estimates on
(\bar{\phi}^{k+1},\bar{\psi}^{k+1})
.
Actually, by the standard energy estimates for hyperbolic equations,
\eqref{k+3}_{1}
and
\eqref{k+3}_{4}
yield that

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}\|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\|^{2}_{2}\leq&C\sigma^{-1}\|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\|^{2}_{2}+\sigma\|\nabla\bar{u}^{k}\|^{2}_{1}+C|\nabla^{3}\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}|_{D^{2}},\\
\frac{d}{dt}\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|^{2}_{1}\leq&C\sigma^{-1}\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|^{2}_{1}+\sigma(|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+\|\bar{\psi}^{k}\|_{1}^{2}\\
&+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2})+C|h^{k}\nabla^{3}\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}|\nabla\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2},\end{split}

(3.125)

where
\sigma\in\big(0,\min\{1,\frac{a_{4}}{32},\frac{a_{2}\alpha}{32}\}\big)
is a constant to be determined later.

Step 1.2:
Estimates on
\bar{l}^{k+1}
.
Multiplying
\eqref{k+3}_{3}
by
\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
yield that

\begin{split}\frac{a_{4}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}+|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{5}N_{i},\end{split}

(3.126)

where
N_{i},i=1,2,\cdots,5
are defined and estimated as follows.

\displaystyle N_{1}=

\displaystyle\int\Big(\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{1}-a_{4}\big((l^{k})^{\nu}\nabla\sqrt{h^{k+1}}+\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla(l^{k})^{\nu}\big)\cdot\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}\Big)\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}

\displaystyle+\int\frac{a_{4}}{2}\Big(\big(\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\big)_{t}(l^{k})^{\nu}+\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\big((l^{k})^{\nu}\big)_{t}\Big)\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}\cdot\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2}+|(l^{k})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}\Big)\big|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+|l^{k}_{t}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{D^{2}})|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2},

(3.127)

\displaystyle N_{2}=

\displaystyle\int\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{2}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}\leq C\big(|(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}|\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{3}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2}|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}\big),

\displaystyle N_{3}=

\displaystyle\int\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{3}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}\leq C\big(|h^{k}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|_{6}+|\bar{\psi}^{k}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2}+|(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}\big)|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2},

\displaystyle N_{4}=

\displaystyle\int\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{4}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}\leq C\Big(\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}+|(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}\Big)|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2},

\begin{split}N_{5}=&\int\bar{\Pi}^{k+1}\cdot\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}\leq C|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}(|(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}\\
&+|(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k}|_{2}),\end{split}

(3.128)

where one has used the facts that

\begin{split}&|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}-\sqrt{h^{k}}|_{6}\leq C|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2},\quad|h^{k+1}(h^{k})^{-1}|_{\infty}+|h^{k}(h^{k+1})^{-1}|_{\infty}\leq C,\\
&|\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{3}\leq C|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{2}|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{6},\\
&|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}\big((h^{k})^{\frac{3}{2}}-(h^{k-1})^{\frac{3}{2}}\big)\nabla u^{k-1}\cdot\nabla u^{k-1}|_{2}\\
\leq&|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}\big((h^{k})^{\frac{3}{4}}-(h^{k-1})^{\frac{3}{4}}\big)\big((h^{k})^{\frac{3}{4}}+(h^{k-1})^{\frac{3}{4}}\big)\nabla u^{k-1}\cdot\nabla u^{k-1}|_{2}\\
\leq&C|\bar{h}^{k}|_{6}|h^{k-1}\nabla u^{k-1}|_{6}|\nabla u^{k-1}|_{6},\\
&a^{-b}\nabla\bar{n}^{k+1}=b((h^{k+1})^{b-1}-(h^{k})^{b-1})\psi^{k+1}+b(h^{k})^{b-1}\bar{\psi}^{k+1},\\
&a^{-b}|\bar{n}^{k+1}|_{6}=|(h^{k+1})^{b}-(h^{k})^{b}|_{6}\leq C|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2},\\
&(h^{k})^{-\frac{3}{2}}-(h^{k-1})^{-\frac{3}{2}}=((h^{k})^{-\frac{3}{4}}-(h^{k-1})^{-\frac{3}{4}})((h^{k})^{-\frac{3}{4}}+(h^{k-1})^{-\frac{3}{4}}).\end{split}

(3.129)

It is noted that the second estimate in
\eqref{errorjiqiao}_{2}
follows from Lemma
3.4
.
Then according to (
3.126
)-(
3.128
), one has

\begin{split}&\frac{a_{4}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}+|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}\\
\leq&C\big((1+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}l^{k}_{t}|_{2})|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}+\sigma^{-3}|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}\\
&+\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|^{2}_{1}\big)+\sigma(|\bar{\psi}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|(l^{k})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}\\
&+|(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}).\end{split}

(3.130)

Next, applying
\partial_{t}
to
\eqref{k+3}_{3}
yields

\displaystyle(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{tt}-a_{4}(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{2}}(l^{k})^{\nu}\triangle\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}_{t}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}+a_{4}(\sqrt{h^{k+1}}(l^{k})^{\nu})_{t}\triangle\bar{l}^{k+1}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}(\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{i})_{t}+\bar{\Pi}_{t}^{k+1}.

Then multiplying the above equation by
\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, one has

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+a_{4}|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}=\sum\limits_{i=6}^{11}N_{i}.

(3.131)

Here
N_{i}
,
i=6,7,\cdots,11
are given and estimated as follows:

\displaystyle N_{6}=

\displaystyle\int\Big(-\frac{1}{2}((h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}(\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1})^{2}+(\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{1})_{t}\Big)\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}

(3.132)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}^{2}+C\Big(|(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}+|(l^{k})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}+|(l^{k})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}^{k}|_{2}+|\bar{u}^{k}|_{\infty}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2}\Big)|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}+C\Big(|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2}(1+|(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}l^{k}_{tt}|_{2})\Big)|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{3},

\displaystyle N_{7}=

\displaystyle\int\Big((\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{2})_{t}+a_{4}\big(\sqrt{h^{k+1}}(l^{k})^{\nu}\big)_{t}\triangle\bar{l}^{k+1}\Big)\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}

(3.133)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big((1+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}l^{k}_{t}|_{2})\big(|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}+\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}\big)+|(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k}_{t}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla^{2}\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}+|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}_{t}|_{2}\Big)|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C(|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}l^{k}_{t}|_{2}|\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}+|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla^{2}\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2})|\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{3},

\displaystyle N_{8}=

\displaystyle\int(\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{3})_{t}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}\leq C\Big(|h^{k}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|_{6}+|\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k}_{t}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}_{t}|_{2}+\big(1+|(h^{k-1}\nabla^{2}u^{k-1}_{t},h^{k}\nabla^{2}u^{k}_{t})|_{2}\big)\big(|(h^{k})^{\frac{3}{4}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|_{3}

\displaystyle+|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k}|_{2}\big)\Big)|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}_{t}|_{2}|\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{3},

\displaystyle N_{9}=

\displaystyle\int(\bar{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{4})_{t}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}\leq C\Big(\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}+|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}+|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}_{t}|_{2}\Big)|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}+N_{*},

\displaystyle N_{10}=

\displaystyle\int\bar{\Pi}_{t}^{k+1}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}\leq C\Big(\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k}_{t}|_{2}+\|\bar{\psi}^{k}\|_{1}

\displaystyle+(1+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}l^{k}_{t}|_{2})(|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}\bar{l}^{k}|_{2})

\displaystyle+|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}_{t}|_{2}+(1+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}l^{k}_{t}|_{2})|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k}_{t}|_{2}\Big)|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}+C(\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}+|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2})|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2},

\displaystyle N_{11}=

\displaystyle-a_{4}\int\big(\nabla\sqrt{h^{k+1}}(l^{k})^{\nu}\cdot\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}+\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla(l^{k})^{\nu}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}\big)\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq C|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2},

where one has used (
3.129
). By
\eqref{k+3}_{4}
, the remaining term
N_{*}
in
N_{9}
can be treated as follows by the integration by parts,

\begin{split}N_{*}=&a_{6}\int(l^{k})^{\nu+1}(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}\\
=&-a_{6}\int(l^{k})^{\nu+1}(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}\text{div}\Big(\nabla(u^{k}\cdot\bar{\psi}^{k+1})+\nabla(\bar{u}^{k}\cdot\psi^{k})\\
&+(\delta-1)(\bar{\psi}^{k}\text{div}u^{k}+\psi^{k-1}\text{div}\bar{u}^{k})+a\delta(h^{k}\nabla\text{div}\bar{u}^{k}+\bar{h}^{k}\nabla\text{div}u^{k-1})\Big)\\
\leq&C\Big(\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}+\|\bar{\psi}^{k}\|_{1}+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}\\
&+|h^{k}\nabla^{3}\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}\Big)|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}+C\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}.\end{split}

(3.134)

Moreover,
\eqref{k+3}_{4}
implies that

\begin{split}|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}\leq&C\big(\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}+\|\bar{u}^{k}\|_{1}+|\bar{\psi}^{k}|_{2}+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}\big).\end{split}

(3.135)

Then collecting estimates (
3.131
)-(
3.135
) yields that

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+\frac{a_{4}}{2}|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}\\
\leq&C\sigma^{-2}(1+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}l^{k}_{t}|^{2}_{2}+|(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}l^{k}_{tt}|^{2}_{2}+|h^{k-1}\nabla^{2}u^{k-1}_{t}|^{2}_{2}+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}u^{k}_{t}|^{2}_{2})\\
&(\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|^{2}_{1}+|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2})+\sigma(\|\bar{\psi}^{k}\|^{2}_{1}\\
&+|(l^{k})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|(l^{k})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k}_{t}|^{2}_{2}\\
&+(1+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}l^{k}_{t}|_{2})|(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla^{2}\bar{l}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla^{2}\bar{l}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}\\
&+\|\bar{u}^{k-1}\|^{2}_{1}+|\bar{\psi}^{k-1}|^{2}_{2}+|h^{k-1}\nabla^{2}\bar{u}^{k-1}|^{2}_{2})+C\widetilde{\epsilon}^{-2}|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}\\
&+\widetilde{\epsilon}(|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k}_{t}|^{2}_{2}+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}_{t}|^{2}_{2})+C|h^{k}\nabla^{3}\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2},\end{split}

(3.136)

where
\widetilde{\epsilon}\in(0,1)
is a constant to be determined later.

Step 1.3:
Estimates on
\bar{u}^{k+1}
.
First, multiplying
\eqref{k+3}_{2}
by
2\bar{u}^{k+1}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
yield that

\begin{split}&\frac{d}{dt}|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+a_{2}\alpha|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}\\
\leq&C\sigma^{-1}(1+|\nabla^{2}l^{k+1}_{t}|_{2})|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+\sigma(|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}\\
&+|\bar{\psi}^{k}|^{2}_{2})+C(\|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\|^{2}_{1}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+|\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}).\end{split}

(3.137)

Second, multiplying
\eqref{k+3}_{2}
by
2\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
give that

\begin{split}&|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{d}{dt}a_{2}\alpha|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}\\
\leq&C(|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+\|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\|^{2}_{1}+|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}\\
&+\sigma^{-1}|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}^{k+1}_{t}|^{2}_{2})+\sigma(|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|^{2}_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k}|^{2}_{2}).\end{split}

(3.138)

Next, applying
\partial_{t}
to
\eqref{k+3}_{2}
gives

\displaystyle(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}\bar{u}_{tt}^{k+1}+a_{2}h^{k+1}L\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-((l^{k+1})^{-\nu})_{t}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}-a_{2}h^{k+1}_{t}L\bar{u}^{k+1}-a_{2}(\bar{h}^{k+1}Lu^{k})_{t}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}(\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{i})_{t}.

Then multiplying above system by
2\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
lead to

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+2a_{2}\alpha|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}

(3.139)

\displaystyle+2a_{2}(\alpha+\beta)|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\text{div}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{5}O_{i},

where
O_{i},i=1,2,\cdots,5
are given and estimated as follows:

\displaystyle O_{1}=

\displaystyle\int(-(l^{k+1})^{-\nu})_{t}(\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1})^{2}+(2\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{1})_{t}\cdot\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1})

(3.140)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|l^{k+1}_{t}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{D^{2}}|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|^{2}_{2}+C\big(|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}^{k}|_{2}+|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\bar{u}^{k}\|_{1}+(1+|u^{k}_{tt}|_{2})|(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}\big)|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+|u^{k}_{tt}|_{2})|(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2},

\displaystyle\displaystyle O_{2}=

\displaystyle\int-2a_{2}\big(\nabla h^{k+1}\cdot Q(\bar{u}^{k+1}_{t})+h^{k+1}_{t}L\bar{u}^{k+1}+(\bar{h}^{k+1}Lu^{k})_{t}\big)\cdot\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}+|\nabla^{2}\bar{u}^{k+1}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\nabla^{2}u^{k}_{t}|_{2}\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}\big)|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2},

\displaystyle O_{3}=

\displaystyle\int 2(\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{2})_{t}\cdot\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}\leq C\big(\|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\|_{2}+\|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}_{t}\|_{1}+|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+(1+|l^{k}_{t}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{D^{2}}+|l^{k+1}_{t}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{D^{2}})|(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}\big)|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2},

\displaystyle\displaystyle O_{4}=

\displaystyle\int 2(\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{3})_{t}\cdot\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}\leq C\big((1+|l^{k+1}_{t}|_{D^{2}}

\displaystyle+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}u^{k}_{t}|_{2})|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}+|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+(1+|l^{k}_{t}|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{D^{2}})(\|\nabla\bar{u}^{k}\|_{1}+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}+\|\bar{\psi}^{k}\|_{1})

\displaystyle+|\sqrt{h^{k}}\nabla\bar{u}^{k}_{t}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k}_{t}|_{2}+|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}_{t}|_{2}+\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}\big)|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C((1+|h^{k}\nabla^{2}u^{k}_{t}|_{2})|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}+\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1})|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2},

\displaystyle\displaystyle O_{5}=

\displaystyle\int 2(\bar{\mathcal{U}}^{k+1}_{4})_{t}\cdot\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}\leq C|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}|\sqrt{h^{k+1}}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\big(|(h^{k+1})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}+|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{4}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}\big)|(l^{k+1})^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2},

where one has used integration by parts in
O_{3}
and

to deal with the corresponding terms related to
